Implementation

dataLayer

// E3's recommended practice (D-63): one namespaced 'bloomreach' object on the site's existing
// event, so GA4 and other keys on the dataLayer root are never touched. The client may structure
// the dataLayer differently; validation always runs on the Bloomreach event payload, not here.
window.dataLayer = window.dataLayer || [];
window.dataLayer.push({ bloomreach: null }); // reset, so nothing leaks from the previous event
window.dataLayer.push({
  event: 'Opt_in_proof', // the site's dataLayer event carrying this Bloomreach event (set dl_event_name in the plan)
  bloomreach: {
    event: 'Opt_in_proof',
    properties: {
      // Optional:
      category: 'string',
      identification_type: 'string',
      message: 'string',
      source: 'string',
      updated at: 1735689600,
    },
  },
});

GTM custom template

// E3 shared transformation, version 1. A fixed artefact: do not edit per client.
// Ships as the E3 GTM custom template and as this JS helper.
function brPayload(br, rules) {
  var out = {};
  if (!br) return out;
  var props = br.properties || {};
  var item = br.item || {};
  var items = br.items || [];
  function defined(v) { return v !== undefined && v !== null; }
  Object.keys(props).forEach(function (k) { out[k] = props[k]; });
  Object.keys(item).forEach(function (k) { out[k] = item[k]; });
  (rules || []).forEach(function (r) {
    if (defined(props[r.name])) return; // a supplied value wins over the computed one
    if (r.sum) {
      var fields = r.sum.split('*').map(function (f) { return f.trim(); }).filter(Boolean);
      var total = 0;
      items.forEach(function (it) {
        var product = 1;
        fields.forEach(function (f) { product *= Number(it[f]) || 0; });
        total += product;
      });
      out[r.name] = Math.round(total * 1e6) / 1e6;
    } else if (r.form === 'ids') {
      out[r.name] = items.map(function (i) { return i[r.id_field]; }).filter(defined);
    } else {
      var q = r.quantity_field || 'quantity';
      out[r.name] = items.filter(function (i) { return defined(i[r.id_field]); }).map(function (i) {
        var o = {};
        o[r.id_field] = i[r.id_field];
        o.quantity = i[q]; // output key is always quantity
        return o;
      });
    }
  });
  return out;
}

// This event's state lists and totals (from the property kinds in the plan):
var BR_RULES = [];

// GTM custom template setup
//  1. Trigger: Custom Event "Opt_in_proof", fires only when the Data Layer Variable "bloomreach" is defined.
//  2. Tag: the E3 Bloomreach tag (custom template) reads the Data Layer Variable "bloomreach".
//  3. The tag calls the Web SDK: bloomreach.track('Opt_in_proof', brPayload(dataLayer.bloomreach, BR_RULES));
//  4. One tag serves every event (it reads bloomreach.event); only the trigger differs per site event.

Bloomreach Web SDK

// Final payload, as brPayload produces it from the dataLayer object.
bloomreach.track('Opt_in_proof', {
  // Optional:
  category: 'string',
  identification_type: 'string',
  message: 'string',
  source: 'string',
  updated at: 1735689600,
});

Bloomreach Web SDK (legacy)

// Final payload, as brPayload produces it from the dataLayer object.
exponea.track('Opt_in_proof', {
  // Optional:
  category: 'string',
  identification_type: 'string',
  message: 'string',
  source: 'string',
  updated at: 1735689600,
});

Bloomreach Tracking API

// Optional properties: category, identification_type, message, source, updated at

{
  "customer_ids": {
    "registered": "<customer_id>"
  },
  "event_type": "Opt_in_proof",
  "properties": {
    "category": "string",
    "identification_type": "string",
    "message": "string",
    "source": "string",
    "updated at": 1735689600
  }
}

// cURL example:
curl -X POST "https://api.exponea.com/track/v2/projects/YOUR_PROJECT_TOKEN/customers/events" \
  -H "Authorization: Basic YOUR_BASE64_API_KEY" \
  -H "Content-Type: application/json" \
  -d '{"customer_ids":{"registered":"<customer_id>"},"event_type":"Opt_in_proof","properties":{"category":"string","identification_type":"string","message":"string","source":"string","updated at":1735689600}}'

// E3 shared transformation, version 1. A fixed artefact: do not edit per client.
// Ships as the E3 GTM custom template and as this JS helper.
function brPayload(br, rules) {
  var out = {};
  if (!br) return out;
  var props = br.properties || {};
  var item = br.item || {};
  var items = br.items || [];
  function defined(v) { return v !== undefined && v !== null; }
  Object.keys(props).forEach(function (k) { out[k] = props[k]; });
  Object.keys(item).forEach(function (k) { out[k] = item[k]; });
  (rules || []).forEach(function (r) {
    if (defined(props[r.name])) return; // a supplied value wins over the computed one
    if (r.sum) {
      var fields = r.sum.split('*').map(function (f) { return f.trim(); }).filter(Boolean);
      var total = 0;
      items.forEach(function (it) {
        var product = 1;
        fields.forEach(function (f) { product *= Number(it[f]) || 0; });
        total += product;
      });
      out[r.name] = Math.round(total * 1e6) / 1e6;
    } else if (r.form === 'ids') {
      out[r.name] = items.map(function (i) { return i[r.id_field]; }).filter(defined);
    } else {
      var q = r.quantity_field || 'quantity';
      out[r.name] = items.filter(function (i) { return defined(i[r.id_field]); }).map(function (i) {
        var o = {};
        o[r.id_field] = i[r.id_field];
        o.quantity = i[q]; // output key is always quantity
        return o;
      });
    }
  });
  return out;
}

dataLayer

// E3's recommended practice (D-63): one namespaced 'bloomreach' object on the site's existing
// event, so GA4 and other keys on the dataLayer root are never touched. The client may structure
// the dataLayer differently; validation always runs on the Bloomreach event payload, not here.
window.dataLayer = window.dataLayer || [];
window.dataLayer.push({ bloomreach: null }); // reset, so nothing leaks from the previous event
window.dataLayer.push({
  event: 'banner', // the site's dataLayer event carrying this Bloomreach event (set dl_event_name in the plan)
  bloomreach: {
    event: 'banner',
    properties: {
      // Optional:
      _gl: 'string',
      action: 'string',

      // Optional:

      product_id: '102589',
    },
    // Computed by brPayload from items: product_ids

GTM custom template

// E3 shared transformation, version 1. A fixed artefact: do not edit per client.
// Ships as the E3 GTM custom template and as this JS helper.
function brPayload(br, rules) {
  var out = {};
  if (!br) return out;
  var props = br.properties || {};
  var item = br.item || {};
  var items = br.items || [];
  function defined(v) { return v !== undefined && v !== null; }
  Object.keys(props).forEach(function (k) { out[k] = props[k]; });
  Object.keys(item).forEach(function (k) { out[k] = item[k]; });
  (rules || []).forEach(function (r) {
    if (defined(props[r.name])) return; // a supplied value wins over the computed one
    if (r.sum) {
      var fields = r.sum.split('*').map(function (f) { return f.trim(); }).filter(Boolean);
      var total = 0;
      items.forEach(function (it) {
        var product = 1;
        fields.forEach(function (f) { product *= Number(it[f]) || 0; });
        total += product;
      });
      out[r.name] = Math.round(total * 1e6) / 1e6;
    } else if (r.form === 'ids') {
      out[r.name] = items.map(function (i) { return i[r.id_field]; }).filter(defined);
    } else {
      var q = r.quantity_field || 'quantity';
      out[r.name] = items.filter(function (i) { return defined(i[r.id_field]); }).map(function (i) {
        var o = {};
        o[r.id_field] = i[r.id_field];
        o.quantity = i[q]; // output key is always quantity
        return o;
      });
    }
  });
  return out;
}

// This event's state lists and totals (from the property kinds in the plan):
var BR_RULES = [
  {"name":"product_ids","form":"ids","id_field":"product_id"},
];

// GTM custom template setup
//  1. Trigger: Custom Event "banner", fires only when the Data Layer Variable "bloomreach" is defined.
//  2. Tag: the E3 Bloomreach tag (custom template) reads the Data Layer Variable "bloomreach".
//  3. The tag calls the Web SDK: bloomreach.track('banner', brPayload(dataLayer.bloomreach, BR_RULES));
//  4. One tag serves every event (it reads bloomreach.event); only the trigger differs per site event.

// E3's recommended practice (D-63): one namespaced 'bloomreach' object on the site's existing
// event, so GA4 and other keys on the dataLayer root are never touched. The client may structure
// the dataLayer differently; validation always runs on the Bloomreach event payload, not here.
window.dataLayer = window.dataLayer || [];
window.dataLayer.push({ bloomreach: null }); // reset, so nothing leaks from the previous event
window.dataLayer.push({
  event: 'booking_b', // the site's dataLayer event carrying this Bloomreach event (set dl_event_name in the plan)
  bloomreach: {

GTM custom template

// E3 shared transformation, version 1. A fixed artefact: do not edit per client.
// Ships as the E3 GTM custom template and as this JS helper.
function brPayload(br, rules) {
  var out = {};
  if (!br) return out;
  var props = br.properties || {};
  var item = br.item || {};
  var items = br.items || [];
  function defined(v) { return v !== undefined && v !== null; }
  Object.keys(props).forEach(function (k) { out[k] = props[k]; });
  Object.keys(item).forEach(function (k) { out[k] = item[k]; });
  (rules || []).forEach(function (r) {
    if (defined(props[r.name])) return; // a supplied value wins over the computed one
    if (r.sum) {
      var fields = r.sum.split('*').map(function (f) { return f.trim(); }).filter(Boolean);
      var total = 0;
      items.forEach(function (it) {
        var product = 1;
        fields.forEach(function (f) { product *= Number(it[f]) || 0; });
        total += product;
      });
      out[r.name] = Math.round(total * 1e6) / 1e6;
    } else if (r.form === 'ids') {
      out[r.name] = items.map(function (i) { return i[r.id_field]; }).filter(defined);
    } else {
      var q = r.quantity_field || 'quantity';
      out[r.name] = items.filter(function (i) { return defined(i[r.id_field]); }).map(function (i) {
        var o = {};
        o[r.id_field] = i[r.id_field];
        o.quantity = i[q]; // output key is always quantity
        return o;
      });
    }
  });
  return out;
}

// This event's state lists and totals (from the property kinds in the plan):
var BR_RULES = [];

// GTM custom template setup
//  1. Trigger: Custom Event "booking_b", fires only when the Data Layer Variable "bloomreach" is defined.
//  2. Tag: the E3 Bloomreach tag (custom template) reads the Data Layer Variable "bloomreach".
//  3. The tag calls the Web SDK: bloomreach.track('booking_b', brPayload(dataLayer.bloomreach, BR_RULES));
//  4. One tag serves every event (it reads bloomreach.event); only the trigger differs per site event.

Implementation

dataLayer

// E3's recommended practice (D-63): one namespaced 'bloomreach' object on the site's existing
// event, so GA4 and other keys on the dataLayer root are never touched. The client may structure
// the dataLayer differently; validation always runs on the Bloomreach event payload, not here.
window.dataLayer = window.dataLayer || [];
window.dataLayer.push({ bloomreach: null }); // reset, so nothing leaks from the previous event
window.dataLayer.push({
  event: 'campaign', // the site's dataLayer event carrying this Bloomreach event (set dl_event_name in the plan)
  bloomreach: {
    event: 'campaign',

GTM custom template

// E3 shared transformation, version 1. A fixed artefact: do not edit per client.
// Ships as the E3 GTM custom template and as this JS helper.
function brPayload(br, rules) {
  var out = {};
  if (!br) return out;
  var props = br.properties || {};
  var item = br.item || {};
  var items = br.items || [];
  function defined(v) { return v !== undefined && v !== null; }
  Object.keys(props).forEach(function (k) { out[k] = props[k]; });
  Object.keys(item).forEach(function (k) { out[k] = item[k]; });
  (rules || []).forEach(function (r) {
    if (defined(props[r.name])) return; // a supplied value wins over the computed one
    if (r.sum) {
      var fields = r.sum.split('*').map(function (f) { return f.trim(); }).filter(Boolean);
      var total = 0;
      items.forEach(function (it) {
        var product = 1;
        fields.forEach(function (f) { product *= Number(it[f]) || 0; });
        total += product;
      });
      out[r.name] = Math.round(total * 1e6) / 1e6;
    } else if (r.form === 'ids') {
      out[r.name] = items.map(function (i) { return i[r.id_field]; }).filter(defined);
    } else {
      var q = r.quantity_field || 'quantity';
      out[r.name] = items.filter(function (i) { return defined(i[r.id_field]); }).map(function (i) {
        var o = {};
        o[r.id_field] = i[r.id_field];
        o.quantity = i[q]; // output key is always quantity
        return o;
      });
    }
  });
  return out;
}

// This event's state lists and totals (from the property kinds in the plan):
var BR_RULES = [
  {"name":"product_ids","form":"ids","id_field":"product_id"},
  {"name":"product_list","form":"id_quantity","id_field":"product_id","quantity_field":"quantity"},
  {"name":"total_price","sum":"price*quantity"},
];

// GTM custom template setup
//  1. Trigger: Custom Event "campaign", fires only when the Data Layer Variable "bloomreach" is defined.
//  2. Tag: the E3 Bloomreach tag (custom template) reads the Data Layer Variable "bloomreach".
//  3. The tag calls the Web SDK: bloomreach.track('campaign', brPayload(dataLayer.bloomreach, BR_RULES));
//  4. One tag serves every event (it reads bloomreach.event); only the trigger differs per site event.

Bloomreach Web SDK

// Final payload, as brPayload produces it from the dataLayer object.
bloomreach.track('campaign', {
  // Optional:

// cURL example:
curl -X POST "https://api.exponea.com/track/v2/projects/YOUR_PROJECT_TOKEN/customers/events" \
  -H "Authorization: Basic YOUR_BASE64_API_KEY" \

Sample payload

  "action": "string",
  "product_id": "102589",
  "total_price": 123,
  "total_quantity": 123,
  "brand": "string",
  "product_ids": "string",
  "product_list": "string",
  "size": "string",
  "title": "string"
}

Implementation

dataLayer

// E3's recommended practice (D-63): one namespaced 'bloomreach' object on the site's existing
// event, so GA4 and other keys on the dataLayer root are never touched. The client may structure
// the dataLayer differently; validation always runs on the Bloomreach event payload, not here.
window.dataLayer = window.dataLayer || [];
window.dataLayer.push({ bloomreach: null }); // reset, so nothing leaks from the previous event
window.dataLayer.push({
  event: 'add_to_cart', // the site's existing dataLayer event, unchanged
  bloomreach: {
    event: 'cart_update',
    properties: {
      // Optional:
      action: 'string',
      brand: 'string',
      size: 'string',
      title: 'string',
      total_quantity: 123,
    },
    item: {
      // Optional:
      product_id: '102589',
    },
    // Computed by brPayload from items: product_ids, product_list, total_price
    // (put a value in properties to override a computed one)
    items: [ // the whole collection after the action, one entry per line
      {
        product_id: '102589',
        quantity: 1,
        price: 123,
      },
    ],
  },
});

GTM custom template

// E3 shared transformation, version 1. A fixed artefact: do not edit per client.
// Ships as the E3 GTM custom template and as this JS helper.
function brPayload(br, rules) {
  var out = {};
  if (!br) return out;
  var props = br.properties || {};
  var item = br.item || {};
  var items = br.items || [];
  function defined(v) { return v !== undefined && v !== null; }
  Object.keys(props).forEach(function (k) { out[k] = props[k]; });
  Object.keys(item).forEach(function (k) { out[k] = item[k]; });
  (rules || []).forEach(function (r) {
    if (defined(props[r.name])) return; // a supplied value wins over the computed one
    if (r.sum) {
      var fields = r.sum.split('*').map(function (f) { return f.trim(); }).filter(Boolean);
      var total = 0;
      items.forEach(function (it) {
        var product = 1;
        fields.forEach(function (f) { product *= Number(it[f]) || 0; });
        total += product;
      });
      out[r.name] = Math.round(total * 1e6) / 1e6;
    } else if (r.form === 'ids') {
      out[r.name] = items.map(function (i) { return i[r.id_field]; }).filter(defined);
    } else {
      var q = r.quantity_field || 'quantity';
      out[r.name] = items.filter(function (i) { return defined(i[r.id_field]); }).map(function (i) {
        var o = {};
        o[r.id_field] = i[r.id_field];
        o.quantity = i[q]; // output key is always quantity
        return o;
      });
    }
  });
  return out;
}

// This event's state lists and totals (from the property kinds in the plan):
var BR_RULES = [
  {"name":"product_ids","form":"ids","id_field":"product_id"},
  {"name":"product_list","form":"id_quantity","id_field":"product_id","quantity_field":"quantity"},
  {"name":"total_price","sum":"price*quantity"},
];

// GTM custom template setup
//  1. Trigger: Custom Event "add_to_cart", fires only when the Data Layer Variable "bloomreach" is defined.
//  2. Tag: the E3 Bloomreach tag (custom template) reads the Data Layer Variable "bloomreach".
//  3. The tag calls the Web SDK: bloomreach.track('cart_update', brPayload(dataLayer.bloomreach, BR_RULES));
//  4. One tag serves every event (it reads bloomreach.event); only the trigger differs per site event.

Bloomreach Web SDK

// Final payload, as brPayload produces it from the dataLayer object.
bloomreach.track('cart_update', {
  // Optional:
  action: 'string',
  brand: 'string',
  product_id: '102589',
  product_ids: ['102589'],
  product_list: [{
      product_id: '102589',
      quantity: 1
    }],
  size: 'string',
  title: 'string',
  total_price: 123,
  total_quantity: 123,
});

// E3's recommended practice (D-63): one namespaced 'bloomreach' object on the site's existing
// event, so GA4 and other keys on the dataLayer root are never touched. The client may structure
// the dataLayer differently; validation always runs on the Bloomreach event payload, not here.
window.dataLayer = window.dataLayer || [];
window.dataLayer.push({ bloomreach: null }); // reset, so nothing leaks from the previous event
window.dataLayer.push({

GTM custom template

// E3 shared transformation, version 1. A fixed artefact: do not edit per client.
// Ships as the E3 GTM custom template and as this JS helper.
function brPayload(br, rules) {
  var out = {};
  if (!br) return out;
  var props = br.properties || {};
  var item = br.item || {};
  var items = br.items || [];
  function defined(v) { return v !== undefined && v !== null; }
  Object.keys(props).forEach(function (k) { out[k] = props[k]; });
  Object.keys(item).forEach(function (k) { out[k] = item[k]; });
  (rules || []).forEach(function (r) {
    if (defined(props[r.name])) return; // a supplied value wins over the computed one
    if (r.sum) {
      var fields = r.sum.split('*').map(function (f) { return f.trim(); }).filter(Boolean);
      var total = 0;
      items.forEach(function (it) {
        var product = 1;
        fields.forEach(function (f) { product *= Number(it[f]) || 0; });
        total += product;
      });
      out[r.name] = Math.round(total * 1e6) / 1e6;
    } else if (r.form === 'ids') {
      out[r.name] = items.map(function (i) { return i[r.id_field]; }).filter(defined);
    } else {
      var q = r.quantity_field || 'quantity';
      out[r.name] = items.filter(function (i) { return defined(i[r.id_field]); }).map(function (i) {
        var o = {};
        o[r.id_field] = i[r.id_field];
        o.quantity = i[q]; // output key is always quantity
        return o;
      });
    }
  });
  return out;
}

// This event's state lists and totals (from the property kinds in the plan):
var BR_RULES = [];

// GTM custom template setup
//  1. Trigger: Custom Event "consent", fires only when the Data Layer Variable "bloomreach" is defined.
//  2. Tag: the E3 Bloomreach tag (custom template) reads the Data Layer Variable "bloomreach".
//  3. The tag calls the Web SDK: bloomreach.track('consent', brPayload(dataLayer.bloomreach, BR_RULES));
//  4. One tag serves every event (it reads bloomreach.event); only the trigger differs per site event.

// E3's recommended practice (D-63): one namespaced 'bloomreach' object on the site's existing
// event, so GA4 and other keys on the dataLayer root are never touched. The client may structure
// the dataLayer differently; validation always runs on the Bloomreach event payload, not here.
window.dataLayer = window.dataLayer || [];
window.dataLayer.push({ bloomreach: null }); // reset, so nothing leaks from the previous event
window.dataLayer.push({
  event: 'double_opt_in', // the site's dataLayer event carrying this Bloomreach event (set dl_event_name in the plan)

GTM custom template

// E3 shared transformation, version 1. A fixed artefact: do not edit per client.
// Ships as the E3 GTM custom template and as this JS helper.
function brPayload(br, rules) {
  var out = {};
  if (!br) return out;
  var props = br.properties || {};
  var item = br.item || {};
  var items = br.items || [];
  function defined(v) { return v !== undefined && v !== null; }
  Object.keys(props).forEach(function (k) { out[k] = props[k]; });
  Object.keys(item).forEach(function (k) { out[k] = item[k]; });
  (rules || []).forEach(function (r) {
    if (defined(props[r.name])) return; // a supplied value wins over the computed one
    if (r.sum) {
      var fields = r.sum.split('*').map(function (f) { return f.trim(); }).filter(Boolean);
      var total = 0;
      items.forEach(function (it) {
        var product = 1;
        fields.forEach(function (f) { product *= Number(it[f]) || 0; });
        total += product;
      });
      out[r.name] = Math.round(total * 1e6) / 1e6;
    } else if (r.form === 'ids') {
      out[r.name] = items.map(function (i) { return i[r.id_field]; }).filter(defined);
    } else {
      var q = r.quantity_field || 'quantity';
      out[r.name] = items.filter(function (i) { return defined(i[r.id_field]); }).map(function (i) {
        var o = {};
        o[r.id_field] = i[r.id_field];
        o.quantity = i[q]; // output key is always quantity
        return o;
      });
    }
  });
  return out;
}

// This event's state lists and totals (from the property kinds in the plan):
var BR_RULES = [];

// GTM custom template setup
//  1. Trigger: Custom Event "double_opt_in", fires only when the Data Layer Variable "bloomreach" is defined.
//  2. Tag: the E3 Bloomreach tag (custom template) reads the Data Layer Variable "bloomreach".
//  3. The tag calls the Web SDK: bloomreach.track('double_opt_in', brPayload(dataLayer.bloomreach, BR_RULES));
//  4. One tag serves every event (it reads bloomreach.event); only the trigger differs per site event.

      utm_source: 'string',
    },
  },
});

GTM custom template

// E3 shared transformation, version 1. A fixed artefact: do not edit per client.
// Ships as the E3 GTM custom template and as this JS helper.
function brPayload(br, rules) {
  var out = {};
  if (!br) return out;
  var props = br.properties || {};
  var item = br.item || {};
  var items = br.items || [];
  function defined(v) { return v !== undefined && v !== null; }
  Object.keys(props).forEach(function (k) { out[k] = props[k]; });
  Object.keys(item).forEach(function (k) { out[k] = item[k]; });
  (rules || []).forEach(function (r) {
    if (defined(props[r.name])) return; // a supplied value wins over the computed one
    if (r.sum) {
      var fields = r.sum.split('*').map(function (f) { return f.trim(); }).filter(Boolean);
      var total = 0;
      items.forEach(function (it) {
        var product = 1;
        fields.forEach(function (f) { product *= Number(it[f]) || 0; });
        total += product;
      });
      out[r.name] = Math.round(total * 1e6) / 1e6;
    } else if (r.form === 'ids') {
      out[r.name] = items.map(function (i) { return i[r.id_field]; }).filter(defined);
    } else {
      var q = r.quantity_field || 'quantity';
      out[r.name] = items.filter(function (i) { return defined(i[r.id_field]); }).map(function (i) {
        var o = {};
        o[r.id_field] = i[r.id_field];
        o.quantity = i[q]; // output key is always quantity
        return o;
      });
    }
  });
  return out;
}

// E3's recommended practice (D-63): one namespaced 'bloomreach' object on the site's existing
// event, so GA4 and other keys on the dataLayer root are never touched. The client may structure
// the dataLayer differently; validation always runs on the Bloomreach event payload, not here.
window.dataLayer = window.dataLayer || [];
window.dataLayer.push({ bloomreach: null }); // reset, so nothing leaks from the previous event
window.dataLayer.push({
  event: 'experiment', // the site's dataLayer event carrying this Bloomreach event (set dl_event_name in the plan)

dataLayer

// E3's recommended practice (D-63): one namespaced 'bloomreach' object on the site's existing
// event, so GA4 and other keys on the dataLayer root are never touched. The client may structure
// the dataLayer differently; validation always runs on the Bloomreach event payload, not here.
window.dataLayer = window.dataLayer || [];
window.dataLayer.push({ bloomreach: null }); // reset, so nothing leaks from the previous event
window.dataLayer.push({

      packed_date: 123,
      purchase_id: 'string',
    },
  },
});

GTM custom template

// E3 shared transformation, version 1. A fixed artefact: do not edit per client.
// Ships as the E3 GTM custom template and as this JS helper.
function brPayload(br, rules) {
  var out = {};
  if (!br) return out;
  var props = br.properties || {};
  var item = br.item || {};
  var items = br.items || [];
  function defined(v) { return v !== undefined && v !== null; }
  Object.keys(props).forEach(function (k) { out[k] = props[k]; });
  Object.keys(item).forEach(function (k) { out[k] = item[k]; });
  (rules || []).forEach(function (r) {
    if (defined(props[r.name])) return; // a supplied value wins over the computed one
    if (r.sum) {
      var fields = r.sum.split('*').map(function (f) { return f.trim(); }).filter(Boolean);
      var total = 0;
      items.forEach(function (it) {
        var product = 1;
        fields.forEach(function (f) { product *= Number(it[f]) || 0; });
        total += product;
      });
      out[r.name] = Math.round(total * 1e6) / 1e6;
    } else if (r.form === 'ids') {
      out[r.name] = items.map(function (i) { return i[r.id_field]; }).filter(defined);
    } else {
      var q = r.quantity_field || 'quantity';
      out[r.name] = items.filter(function (i) { return defined(i[r.id_field]); }).map(function (i) {
        var o = {};
        o[r.id_field] = i[r.id_field];
        o.quantity = i[q]; // output key is always quantity
        return o;
      });
    }
  });
  return out;
}

// This event's state lists and totals (from the property kinds in the plan):
var BR_RULES = [];

// GTM custom template setup
//  1. Trigger: Custom Event "flyer", fires only when the Data Layer Variable "bloomreach" is defined.
//  2. Tag: the E3 Bloomreach tag (custom template) reads the Data Layer Variable "bloomreach".
//  3. The tag calls the Web SDK: bloomreach.track('flyer', brPayload(dataLayer.bloomreach, BR_RULES));
//  4. One tag serves every event (it reads bloomreach.event); only the trigger differs per site event.

Bloomreach Web SDK

// Final payload, as brPayload produces it from the dataLayer object.
bloomreach.track('flyer', {
  // Optional:
  flyer_name: 'string',
  is_flyer_order: true,
  order_date: 'string',
  packed_date: 123,
  purchase_id: 'string',
});

// E3's recommended practice (D-63): one namespaced 'bloomreach' object on the site's existing
// event, so GA4 and other keys on the dataLayer root are never touched. The client may structure
// the dataLayer differently; validation always runs on the Bloomreach event payload, not here.
window.dataLayer = window.dataLayer || [];
window.dataLayer.push({ bloomreach: null }); // reset, so nothing leaks from the previous event
window.dataLayer.push({

  bloomreach: {
    event: 'managed_endpoint',
    properties: {
      // Optional:

      action: 'string',

      http_status_code: 123,
      http_user_agent: 'string',
      id: 'string',
      name: 'string',
      template_error: 'string',
      template_message: 'string',
    },
  },
});

GTM custom template

// E3 shared transformation, version 1. A fixed artefact: do not edit per client.
// Ships as the E3 GTM custom template and as this JS helper.
function brPayload(br, rules) {
  var out = {};
  if (!br) return out;
  var props = br.properties || {};
  var item = br.item || {};
  var items = br.items || [];
  function defined(v) { return v !== undefined && v !== null; }
  Object.keys(props).forEach(function (k) { out[k] = props[k]; });
  Object.keys(item).forEach(function (k) { out[k] = item[k]; });
  (rules || []).forEach(function (r) {
    if (defined(props[r.name])) return; // a supplied value wins over the computed one
    if (r.sum) {
      var fields = r.sum.split('*').map(function (f) { return f.trim(); }).filter(Boolean);
      var total = 0;
      items.forEach(function (it) {
        var product = 1;
        fields.forEach(function (f) { product *= Number(it[f]) || 0; });
        total += product;
      });
      out[r.name] = Math.round(total * 1e6) / 1e6;
    } else if (r.form === 'ids') {
      out[r.name] = items.map(function (i) { return i[r.id_field]; }).filter(defined);
    } else {
      var q = r.quantity_field || 'quantity';
      out[r.name] = items.filter(function (i) { return defined(i[r.id_field]); }).map(function (i) {
        var o = {};
        o[r.id_field] = i[r.id_field];
        o.quantity = i[q]; // output key is always quantity
        return o;
      });
    }
  });
  return out;
}

// This event's state lists and totals (from the property kinds in the plan):
var BR_RULES = [];

// GTM custom template setup
//  1. Trigger: Custom Event "managed_endpoint", fires only when the Data Layer Variable "bloomreach" is defined.
//  2. Tag: the E3 Bloomreach tag (custom template) reads the Data Layer Variable "bloomreach".
//  3. The tag calls the Web SDK: bloomreach.track('managed_endpoint', brPayload(dataLayer.bloomreach, BR_RULES));
//  4. One tag serves every event (it reads bloomreach.event); only the trigger differs per site event.

Bloomreach Web SDK

// Final payload, as brPayload produces it from the dataLayer object.
bloomreach.track('managed_endpoint', {
  // Optional:
  access_group_name: 'string',
  action: 'string',
  error_type: 'string',
  http_status_code: 123,
  http_user_agent: 'string',
  id: 'string',
  name: 'string',
  template_error: 'string',
  template_message: 'string',
});

  id: 'string',

      var fields = r.sum.split('*').map(function (f) { return f.trim(); }).filter(Boolean);
      var total = 0;
      items.forEach(function (it) {
        var product = 1;
        fields.forEach(function (f) { product *= Number(it[f]) || 0; });
        total += product;
      });
      out[r.name] = Math.round(total * 1e6) / 1e6;
    } else if (r.form === 'ids') {
      out[r.name] = items.map(function (i) { return i[r.id_field]; }).filter(defined);
    } else {
      var q = r.quantity_field || 'quantity';
      out[r.name] = items.filter(function (i) { return defined(i[r.id_field]); }).map(function (i) {
        var o = {};
        o[r.id_field] = i[r.id_field];
        o.quantity = i[q]; // output key is always quantity
        return o;
      });
    }
  });
  return out;
}

Implementation

dataLayer

// E3's recommended practice (D-63): one namespaced 'bloomreach' object on the site's existing
// event, so GA4 and other keys on the dataLayer root are never touched. The client may structure
// the dataLayer differently; validation always runs on the Bloomreach event payload, not here.
window.dataLayer = window.dataLayer || [];
window.dataLayer.push({ bloomreach: null }); // reset, so nothing leaks from the previous event
window.dataLayer.push({
  event: 'optilyz_campaign', // the site's dataLayer event carrying this Bloomreach event (set dl_event_name in the plan)
  bloomreach: {
    event: 'optilyz_campaign',
    properties: {
      // Optional:
      optilyz_campaign_name: 'string',
      optilyz_campaign_status: 'string',
      optilyz_campaign_validation_result: 'string',
      optilyz_campaign_variant: 'string',
      optilyz_unique_voucher: 'string',
    },
  },
});

GTM custom template

// E3 shared transformation, version 1. A fixed artefact: do not edit per client.
// Ships as the E3 GTM custom template and as this JS helper.
function brPayload(br, rules) {
  var out = {};
  if (!br) return out;
  var props = br.properties || {};
  var item = br.item || {};
  var items = br.items || [];
  function defined(v) { return v !== undefined && v !== null; }
  Object.keys(props).forEach(function (k) { out[k] = props[k]; });
  Object.keys(item).forEach(function (k) { out[k] = item[k]; });
  (rules || []).forEach(function (r) {
    if (defined(props[r.name])) return; // a supplied value wins over the computed one
    if (r.sum) {
      var fields = r.sum.split('*').map(function (f) { return f.trim(); }).filter(Boolean);
      var total = 0;
      items.forEach(function (it) {
        var product = 1;
        fields.forEach(function (f) { product *= Number(it[f]) || 0; });
        total += product;
      });
      out[r.name] = Math.round(total * 1e6) / 1e6;
    } else if (r.form === 'ids') {
      out[r.name] = items.map(function (i) { return i[r.id_field]; }).filter(defined);
    } else {
      var q = r.quantity_field || 'quantity';
      out[r.name] = items.filter(function (i) { return defined(i[r.id_field]); }).map(function (i) {
        var o = {};
        o[r.id_field] = i[r.id_field];
        o.quantity = i[q]; // output key is always quantity
        return o;
      });
    }
  });
  return out;
}

dataLayer

// E3's recommended practice (D-63): one namespaced 'bloomreach' object on the site's existing
// event, so GA4 and other keys on the dataLayer root are never touched. The client may structure
// the dataLayer differently; validation always runs on the Bloomreach event payload, not here.
window.dataLayer = window.dataLayer || [];
window.dataLayer.push({ bloomreach: null }); // reset, so nothing leaks from the previous event
window.dataLayer.push({
  event: 'parcel_insert', // the site's dataLayer event carrying this Bloomreach event (set dl_event_name in the plan)
  bloomreach: {
    event: 'parcel_insert',
    properties: {
      // Optional:
      ab_test_variant: 'string',
      aov: 123,
      categories_string: 'string',
      email: 'string',
      has_purchased_sports: true,

GTM custom template

// E3 shared transformation, version 1. A fixed artefact: do not edit per client.
// Ships as the E3 GTM custom template and as this JS helper.
function brPayload(br, rules) {
  var out = {};
  if (!br) return out;
  var props = br.properties || {};
  var item = br.item || {};
  var items = br.items || [];
  function defined(v) { return v !== undefined && v !== null; }
  Object.keys(props).forEach(function (k) { out[k] = props[k]; });
  Object.keys(item).forEach(function (k) { out[k] = item[k]; });
  (rules || []).forEach(function (r) {
    if (defined(props[r.name])) return; // a supplied value wins over the computed one
    if (r.sum) {
      var fields = r.sum.split('*').map(function (f) { return f.trim(); }).filter(Boolean);
      var total = 0;
      items.forEach(function (it) {
        var product = 1;
        fields.forEach(function (f) { product *= Number(it[f]) || 0; });
        total += product;
      });
      out[r.name] = Math.round(total * 1e6) / 1e6;
    } else if (r.form === 'ids') {
      out[r.name] = items.map(function (i) { return i[r.id_field]; }).filter(defined);
    } else {
      var q = r.quantity_field || 'quantity';
      out[r.name] = items.filter(function (i) { return defined(i[r.id_field]); }).map(function (i) {
        var o = {};
        o[r.id_field] = i[r.id_field];
        o.quantity = i[q]; // output key is always quantity
        return o;
      });
    }
  });
  return out;
}

// This event's state lists and totals (from the property kinds in the plan):
var BR_RULES = [];

// GTM custom template setup
//  1. Trigger: Custom Event "parcel_insert", fires only when the Data Layer Variable "bloomreach" is defined.
//  2. Tag: the E3 Bloomreach tag (custom template) reads the Data Layer Variable "bloomreach".
//  3. The tag calls the Web SDK: bloomreach.track('parcel_insert', brPayload(dataLayer.bloomreach, BR_RULES));
//  4. One tag serves every event (it reads bloomreach.event); only the trigger differs per site event.

Implementation

dataLayer

// E3's recommended practice (D-63): one namespaced 'bloomreach' object on the site's existing
// event, so GA4 and other keys on the dataLayer root are never touched. The client may structure
// the dataLayer differently; validation always runs on the Bloomreach event payload, not here.
window.dataLayer = window.dataLayer || [];
window.dataLayer.push({ bloomreach: null }); // reset, so nothing leaks from the previous event
window.dataLayer.push({
  event: 'qbr_metrics', // the site's dataLayer event carrying this Bloomreach event (set dl_event_name in the plan)
  bloomreach: {
    event: 'qbr_metrics',
    properties: {
      // Optional:
      qbr_active_database_news_info: 123,
      qbr_active_news: 123,
      qbr_CLTV: 123,
      qbr_conversion_news_info_subscribers: 123,
      qbr_conversion_newsletter_subscribers: 123,
      qbr_inactive: 123,
      qbr_inactive_database_news_info: 123,
      qbr_one_time_shoppers: 123,

GTM custom template

// E3 shared transformation, version 1. A fixed artefact: do not edit per client.
// Ships as the E3 GTM custom template and as this JS helper.
function brPayload(br, rules) {
  var out = {};
  if (!br) return out;
  var props = br.properties || {};
  var item = br.item || {};
  var items = br.items || [];
  function defined(v) { return v !== undefined && v !== null; }
  Object.keys(props).forEach(function (k) { out[k] = props[k]; });
  Object.keys(item).forEach(function (k) { out[k] = item[k]; });
  (rules || []).forEach(function (r) {
    if (defined(props[r.name])) return; // a supplied value wins over the computed one
    if (r.sum) {
      var fields = r.sum.split('*').map(function (f) { return f.trim(); }).filter(Boolean);
      var total = 0;
      items.forEach(function (it) {
        var product = 1;
        fields.forEach(function (f) { product *= Number(it[f]) || 0; });
        total += product;
      });
      out[r.name] = Math.round(total * 1e6) / 1e6;
    } else if (r.form === 'ids') {
      out[r.name] = items.map(function (i) { return i[r.id_field]; }).filter(defined);
    } else {
      var q = r.quantity_field || 'quantity';
      out[r.name] = items.filter(function (i) { return defined(i[r.id_field]); }).map(function (i) {
        var o = {};
        o[r.id_field] = i[r.id_field];
        o.quantity = i[q]; // output key is always quantity
        return o;
      });
    }
  });
  return out;
}

// This event's state lists and totals (from the property kinds in the plan):
var BR_RULES = [];

// GTM custom template setup
//  1. Trigger: Custom Event "qbr_metrics", fires only when the Data Layer Variable "bloomreach" is defined.
//  2. Tag: the E3 Bloomreach tag (custom template) reads the Data Layer Variable "bloomreach".
//  3. The tag calls the Web SDK: bloomreach.track('qbr_metrics', brPayload(dataLayer.bloomreach, BR_RULES));
//  4. One tag serves every event (it reads bloomreach.event); only the trigger differs per site event.

// E3's recommended practice (D-63): one namespaced 'bloomreach' object on the site's existing
// event, so GA4 and other keys on the dataLayer root are never touched. The client may structure
// the dataLayer differently; validation always runs on the Bloomreach event payload, not here.
window.dataLayer = window.dataLayer || [];
window.dataLayer.push({ bloomreach: null }); // reset, so nothing leaks from the previous event
window.dataLayer.push({
  event: 'received_referrer_reward', // the site's dataLayer event carrying this Bloomreach event (set dl_event_name in the plan)

GTM custom template

// E3 shared transformation, version 1. A fixed artefact: do not edit per client.
// Ships as the E3 GTM custom template and as this JS helper.
function brPayload(br, rules) {
  var out = {};
  if (!br) return out;
  var props = br.properties || {};
  var item = br.item || {};
  var items = br.items || [];
  function defined(v) { return v !== undefined && v !== null; }
  Object.keys(props).forEach(function (k) { out[k] = props[k]; });
  Object.keys(item).forEach(function (k) { out[k] = item[k]; });
  (rules || []).forEach(function (r) {
    if (defined(props[r.name])) return; // a supplied value wins over the computed one
    if (r.sum) {
      var fields = r.sum.split('*').map(function (f) { return f.trim(); }).filter(Boolean);
      var total = 0;
      items.forEach(function (it) {
        var product = 1;
        fields.forEach(function (f) { product *= Number(it[f]) || 0; });
        total += product;
      });
      out[r.name] = Math.round(total * 1e6) / 1e6;
    } else if (r.form === 'ids') {
      out[r.name] = items.map(function (i) { return i[r.id_field]; }).filter(defined);
    } else {
      var q = r.quantity_field || 'quantity';
      out[r.name] = items.filter(function (i) { return defined(i[r.id_field]); }).map(function (i) {
        var o = {};
        o[r.id_field] = i[r.id_field];
        o.quantity = i[q]; // output key is always quantity
        return o;
      });
    }
  });
  return out;
}

// This event's state lists and totals (from the property kinds in the plan):
var BR_RULES = [];

// GTM custom template setup
//  1. Trigger: Custom Event "received_referrer_reward", fires only when the Data Layer Variable "bloomreach" is defined.
//  2. Tag: the E3 Bloomreach tag (custom template) reads the Data Layer Variable "bloomreach".
//  3. The tag calls the Web SDK: bloomreach.track('received_referrer_reward', brPayload(dataLayer.bloomreach, BR_RULES));
//  4. One tag serves every event (it reads bloomreach.event); only the trigger differs per site event.

      referrer_reward: 'string',

GTM custom template

// E3 shared transformation, version 1. A fixed artefact: do not edit per client.
// Ships as the E3 GTM custom template and as this JS helper.
function brPayload(br, rules) {
  var out = {};
  if (!br) return out;
  var props = br.properties || {};
  var item = br.item || {};
  var items = br.items || [];
  function defined(v) { return v !== undefined && v !== null; }
  Object.keys(props).forEach(function (k) { out[k] = props[k]; });
  Object.keys(item).forEach(function (k) { out[k] = item[k]; });
  (rules || []).forEach(function (r) {
    if (defined(props[r.name])) return; // a supplied value wins over the computed one
    if (r.sum) {
      var fields = r.sum.split('*').map(function (f) { return f.trim(); }).filter(Boolean);
      var total = 0;
      items.forEach(function (it) {
        var product = 1;
        fields.forEach(function (f) { product *= Number(it[f]) || 0; });
        total += product;
      });
      out[r.name] = Math.round(total * 1e6) / 1e6;
    } else if (r.form === 'ids') {
      out[r.name] = items.map(function (i) { return i[r.id_field]; }).filter(defined);
    } else {
      var q = r.quantity_field || 'quantity';
      out[r.name] = items.filter(function (i) { return defined(i[r.id_field]); }).map(function (i) {
        var o = {};
        o[r.id_field] = i[r.id_field];
        o.quantity = i[q]; // output key is always quantity
        return o;
      });
    }
  });
  return out;
}

// This event's state lists and totals (from the property kinds in the plan):
var BR_RULES = [];

// GTM custom template setup
//  1. Trigger: Custom Event "registered_as_referee", fires only when the Data Layer Variable "bloomreach" is defined.
//  2. Tag: the E3 Bloomreach tag (custom template) reads the Data Layer Variable "bloomreach".
//  3. The tag calls the Web SDK: bloomreach.track('registered_as_referee', brPayload(dataLayer.bloomreach, BR_RULES));
//  4. One tag serves every event (it reads bloomreach.event); only the trigger differs per site event.

// E3's recommended practice (D-63): one namespaced 'bloomreach' object on the site's existing
// event, so GA4 and other keys on the dataLayer root are never touched. The client may structure
// the dataLayer differently; validation always runs on the Bloomreach event payload, not here.
window.dataLayer = window.dataLayer || [];
window.dataLayer.push({ bloomreach: null }); // reset, so nothing leaks from the previous event
window.dataLayer.push({
  event: 'registered_as_referrer', // the site's dataLayer event carrying this Bloomreach event (set dl_event_name in the plan)

// event, so GA4 and other keys on the dataLayer root are never touched. The client may structure
// the dataLayer differently; validation always runs on the Bloomreach event payload, not here.
window.dataLayer = window.dataLayer || [];
window.dataLayer.push({ bloomreach: null }); // reset, so nothing leaks from the previous event
window.dataLayer.push({
  event: 'return', // the site's dataLayer event carrying this Bloomreach event (set dl_event_name in the plan)
  bloomreach: {
    event: 'return',
    properties: {
      // Optional:
      purchase_status: 'string',
      return_reason: 'string',
      title: 'string',
    },
    item: {
      // Optional:
      price: 123,
      product_id: '102589',
    },
  },
});

GTM custom template

// E3 shared transformation, version 1. A fixed artefact: do not edit per client.
// Ships as the E3 GTM custom template and as this JS helper.
function brPayload(br, rules) {
  var out = {};
  if (!br) return out;
  var props = br.properties || {};
  var item = br.item || {};
  var items = br.items || [];
  function defined(v) { return v !== undefined && v !== null; }
  Object.keys(props).forEach(function (k) { out[k] = props[k]; });
  Object.keys(item).forEach(function (k) { out[k] = item[k]; });
  (rules || []).forEach(function (r) {
    if (defined(props[r.name])) return; // a supplied value wins over the computed one
    if (r.sum) {
      var fields = r.sum.split('*').map(function (f) { return f.trim(); }).filter(Boolean);
      var total = 0;
      items.forEach(function (it) {
        var product = 1;
        fields.forEach(function (f) { product *= Number(it[f]) || 0; });
        total += product;
      });
      out[r.name] = Math.round(total * 1e6) / 1e6;
    } else if (r.form === 'ids') {
      out[r.name] = items.map(function (i) { return i[r.id_field]; }).filter(defined);
    } else {
      var q = r.quantity_field || 'quantity';
      out[r.name] = items.filter(function (i) { return defined(i[r.id_field]); }).map(function (i) {
        var o = {};
        o[r.id_field] = i[r.id_field];
        o.quantity = i[q]; // output key is always quantity
        return o;
      });
    }
  });
  return out;
}

// This event's state lists and totals (from the property kinds in the plan):
var BR_RULES = [];

// GTM custom template setup
//  1. Trigger: Custom Event "return", fires only when the Data Layer Variable "bloomreach" is defined.
//  2. Tag: the E3 Bloomreach tag (custom template) reads the Data Layer Variable "bloomreach".
//  3. The tag calls the Web SDK: bloomreach.track('return', brPayload(dataLayer.bloomreach, BR_RULES));
//  4. One tag serves every event (it reads bloomreach.event); only the trigger differs per site event.

  // Optional:
  price: 123,
  product_id: '102589',
  purchase_status: 'string',
  return_reason: 'string',
  title: 'string',

    "registered": "<customer_id>"
  },
  "event_type": "return",
  "properties": {
    "price": 123,
    "product_id": "102589",

  }

  -H "Authorization: Basic YOUR_BASE64_API_KEY" \
  -H "Content-Type: application/json" \
  -d '{"customer_ids":{"registered":"<customer_id>"},"event_type":"return","properties":{"price":123,"product_id":"102589","purchase_status":"string","return_reason":"string","title":"string"}}'

Emitted by the SDK or the platform itself; there is no tracking snippet.

SDK emits

session_start: Emitted by the Web SDK when a visit starts (SDK initialisation on a page while no session is open). No tracking snippet is needed.

SDK default properties

// Added to every event the SDK tracks (SDK configuration; values below are examples).
track: {
  default_properties: {
    browser: 'Chrome',
    os: 'Android',
  },
},

Implementation

dataLayer

// E3's recommended practice (D-63): one namespaced 'bloomreach' object on the site's existing
// event, so GA4 and other keys on the dataLayer root are never touched. The client may structure
// the dataLayer differently; validation always runs on the Bloomreach event payload, not here.
window.dataLayer = window.dataLayer || [];
window.dataLayer.push({ bloomreach: null }); // reset, so nothing leaks from the previous event
window.dataLayer.push({
  event: 'shipment', // the site's dataLayer event carrying this Bloomreach event (set dl_event_name in the plan)
  bloomreach: {
    event: 'shipment',
    properties: {
      // Optional:
      delivery_date: 'string',
      purchase_date: 'string',
      purchase_id: 'string',
      purchase_status: 'string',
      shipment_status: 'string',
      shipping_city: 'string',
      shipping_cost: 123,
      shipping_country: 'string',
      shipping_date: 'string',
      shipping_postal_code: 123,
      shipping_type: 'string',
    },
  },
});

GTM custom template

// E3 shared transformation, version 1. A fixed artefact: do not edit per client.
// Ships as the E3 GTM custom template and as this JS helper.
function brPayload(br, rules) {
  var out = {};
  if (!br) return out;
  var props = br.properties || {};
  var item = br.item || {};
  var items = br.items || [];
  function defined(v) { return v !== undefined && v !== null; }
  Object.keys(props).forEach(function (k) { out[k] = props[k]; });
  Object.keys(item).forEach(function (k) { out[k] = item[k]; });
  (rules || []).forEach(function (r) {
    if (defined(props[r.name])) return; // a supplied value wins over the computed one
    if (r.sum) {
      var fields = r.sum.split('*').map(function (f) { return f.trim(); }).filter(Boolean);
      var total = 0;
      items.forEach(function (it) {
        var product = 1;
        fields.forEach(function (f) { product *= Number(it[f]) || 0; });
        total += product;
      });
      out[r.name] = Math.round(total * 1e6) / 1e6;
    } else if (r.form === 'ids') {
      out[r.name] = items.map(function (i) { return i[r.id_field]; }).filter(defined);
    } else {
      var q = r.quantity_field || 'quantity';
      out[r.name] = items.filter(function (i) { return defined(i[r.id_field]); }).map(function (i) {
        var o = {};
        o[r.id_field] = i[r.id_field];
        o.quantity = i[q]; // output key is always quantity
        return o;
      });
    }
  });
  return out;
}

// This event's state lists and totals (from the property kinds in the plan):
var BR_RULES = [];

// GTM custom template setup
//  1. Trigger: Custom Event "shipment", fires only when the Data Layer Variable "bloomreach" is defined.
//  2. Tag: the E3 Bloomreach tag (custom template) reads the Data Layer Variable "bloomreach".
//  3. The tag calls the Web SDK: bloomreach.track('shipment', brPayload(dataLayer.bloomreach, BR_RULES));
//  4. One tag serves every event (it reads bloomreach.event); only the trigger differs per site event.

Bloomreach Web SDK

// Final payload, as brPayload produces it from the dataLayer object.
bloomreach.track('shipment', {
  // Optional:
  delivery_date: 'string',
  purchase_date: 'string',
  purchase_id: 'string',
  purchase_status: 'string',
  shipment_status: 'string',
  shipping_city: 'string',
  shipping_cost: 123,
  shipping_country: 'string',
  shipping_date: 'string',
  shipping_postal_code: 123,
  shipping_type: 'string',
});

Bloomreach Web SDK (legacy)

  // Optional:
  delivery_date: 'string',
  purchase_date: 'string',
  purchase_id: 'string',
  purchase_status: 'string',
  shipment_status: 'string',
  shipping_city: 'string',
  shipping_cost: 123,
  shipping_country: 'string',
  shipping_date: 'string',
  shipping_postal_code: 123,
  shipping_type: 'string',

    "registered": "<customer_id>"
  },
  "event_type": "shipment",
  "properties": {
    "delivery_date": "string",
    "purchase_date": "string",
    "purchase_id": "string",
    "purchase_status": "string",

Implementation

dataLayer

// E3's recommended practice (D-63): one namespaced 'bloomreach' object on the site's existing
// event, so GA4 and other keys on the dataLayer root are never touched. The client may structure
// the dataLayer differently; validation always runs on the Bloomreach event payload, not here.
window.dataLayer = window.dataLayer || [];
window.dataLayer.push({ bloomreach: null }); // reset, so nothing leaks from the previous event
window.dataLayer.push({
  event: 'survey_all', // the site's dataLayer event carrying this Bloomreach event (set dl_event_name in the plan)
  bloomreach: {
    event: 'survey_all',
    properties: {
      // Optional:
      answers: [],
      browser: 'x',
      device: 'string',
      first_name: 'string',
      location: 'string',
      os: 'x',
      survey_id: 'string',
      survey_name: 'string',
    },
  },
});

GTM custom template

// E3 shared transformation, version 1. A fixed artefact: do not edit per client.
// Ships as the E3 GTM custom template and as this JS helper.
function brPayload(br, rules) {
  var out = {};
  if (!br) return out;
  var props = br.properties || {};
  var item = br.item || {};
  var items = br.items || [];
  function defined(v) { return v !== undefined && v !== null; }
  Object.keys(props).forEach(function (k) { out[k] = props[k]; });
  Object.keys(item).forEach(function (k) { out[k] = item[k]; });
  (rules || []).forEach(function (r) {
    if (defined(props[r.name])) return; // a supplied value wins over the computed one
    if (r.sum) {
      var fields = r.sum.split('*').map(function (f) { return f.trim(); }).filter(Boolean);
      var total = 0;
      items.forEach(function (it) {
        var product = 1;
        fields.forEach(function (f) { product *= Number(it[f]) || 0; });
        total += product;
      });
      out[r.name] = Math.round(total * 1e6) / 1e6;
    } else if (r.form === 'ids') {
      out[r.name] = items.map(function (i) { return i[r.id_field]; }).filter(defined);
    } else {
      var q = r.quantity_field || 'quantity';
      out[r.name] = items.filter(function (i) { return defined(i[r.id_field]); }).map(function (i) {
        var o = {};
        o[r.id_field] = i[r.id_field];
        o.quantity = i[q]; // output key is always quantity
        return o;
      });
    }
  });
  return out;
}

// This event's state lists and totals (from the property kinds in the plan):
var BR_RULES = [];

// GTM custom template setup
//  1. Trigger: Custom Event "survey_all", fires only when the Data Layer Variable "bloomreach" is defined.
//  2. Tag: the E3 Bloomreach tag (custom template) reads the Data Layer Variable "bloomreach".
//  3. The tag calls the Web SDK: bloomreach.track('survey_all', brPayload(dataLayer.bloomreach, BR_RULES));
//  4. One tag serves every event (it reads bloomreach.event); only the trigger differs per site event.

Bloomreach Web SDK

// Final payload, as brPayload produces it from the dataLayer object.
bloomreach.track('survey_all', {
  // Optional:
  answers: [],
  browser: 'x',
  device: 'string',
  first_name: 'string',
  location: 'string',
  os: 'x',
  survey_id: 'string',
  survey_name: 'string',
});

  location: 'string',
  os: 'x',
  survey_id: 'string',
  survey_name: 'string',

dataLayer

// E3's recommended practice (D-63): one namespaced 'bloomreach' object on the site's existing
// event, so GA4 and other keys on the dataLayer root are never touched. The client may structure
// the dataLayer differently; validation always runs on the Bloomreach event payload, not here.
window.dataLayer = window.dataLayer || [];
window.dataLayer.push({ bloomreach: null }); // reset, so nothing leaks from the previous event
window.dataLayer.push({
  event: 'ticket_registration', // the site's dataLayer event carrying this Bloomreach event (set dl_event_name in the plan)
  bloomreach: {
    event: 'ticket_registration',
    properties: {
      // Optional:
      barcode: 'string',
      delivery_method: 'string',
      email: 'string',
      event_end: 'string',
      event_id: 'string',
      event_name: 'string',
      event_start: 'string',
      first_name: 'string',
      last_name: 'string',
      order_id: 'string',
      ticket_class: 'string',
    },
  },
});

GTM custom template

// E3 shared transformation, version 1. A fixed artefact: do not edit per client.
// Ships as the E3 GTM custom template and as this JS helper.
function brPayload(br, rules) {
  var out = {};
  if (!br) return out;
  var props = br.properties || {};
  var item = br.item || {};
  var items = br.items || [];
  function defined(v) { return v !== undefined && v !== null; }
  Object.keys(props).forEach(function (k) { out[k] = props[k]; });
  Object.keys(item).forEach(function (k) { out[k] = item[k]; });
  (rules || []).forEach(function (r) {
    if (defined(props[r.name])) return; // a supplied value wins over the computed one
    if (r.sum) {
      var fields = r.sum.split('*').map(function (f) { return f.trim(); }).filter(Boolean);
      var total = 0;
      items.forEach(function (it) {
        var product = 1;
        fields.forEach(function (f) { product *= Number(it[f]) || 0; });
        total += product;
      });
      out[r.name] = Math.round(total * 1e6) / 1e6;
    } else if (r.form === 'ids') {
      out[r.name] = items.map(function (i) { return i[r.id_field]; }).filter(defined);
    } else {
      var q = r.quantity_field || 'quantity';
      out[r.name] = items.filter(function (i) { return defined(i[r.id_field]); }).map(function (i) {
        var o = {};
        o[r.id_field] = i[r.id_field];
        o.quantity = i[q]; // output key is always quantity
        return o;
      });
    }
  });
  return out;
}

// This event's state lists and totals (from the property kinds in the plan):
var BR_RULES = [];

// GTM custom template setup
//  1. Trigger: Custom Event "ticket_registration", fires only when the Data Layer Variable "bloomreach" is defined.
//  2. Tag: the E3 Bloomreach tag (custom template) reads the Data Layer Variable "bloomreach".
//  3. The tag calls the Web SDK: bloomreach.track('ticket_registration', brPayload(dataLayer.bloomreach, BR_RULES));
//  4. One tag serves every event (it reads bloomreach.event); only the trigger differs per site event.

Bloomreach Web SDK

// Final payload, as brPayload produces it from the dataLayer object.
bloomreach.track('ticket_registration', {
  // Optional:
  barcode: 'string',
  delivery_method: 'string',
  email: 'string',
  event_end: 'string',
  event_id: 'string',
  event_name: 'string',
  event_start: 'string',
  first_name: 'string',
  last_name: 'string',
  order_id: 'string',
  ticket_class: 'string',
});

GTM custom template

// E3 shared transformation, version 1. A fixed artefact: do not edit per client.
// Ships as the E3 GTM custom template and as this JS helper.
function brPayload(br, rules) {
  var out = {};
  if (!br) return out;
  var props = br.properties || {};
  var item = br.item || {};
  var items = br.items || [];
  function defined(v) { return v !== undefined && v !== null; }
  Object.keys(props).forEach(function (k) { out[k] = props[k]; });
  Object.keys(item).forEach(function (k) { out[k] = item[k]; });
  (rules || []).forEach(function (r) {
    if (defined(props[r.name])) return; // a supplied value wins over the computed one
    if (r.sum) {
      var fields = r.sum.split('*').map(function (f) { return f.trim(); }).filter(Boolean);
      var total = 0;
      items.forEach(function (it) {
        var product = 1;
        fields.forEach(function (f) { product *= Number(it[f]) || 0; });
        total += product;
      });
      out[r.name] = Math.round(total * 1e6) / 1e6;
    } else if (r.form === 'ids') {
      out[r.name] = items.map(function (i) { return i[r.id_field]; }).filter(defined);
    } else {
      var q = r.quantity_field || 'quantity';
      out[r.name] = items.filter(function (i) { return defined(i[r.id_field]); }).map(function (i) {
        var o = {};
        o[r.id_field] = i[r.id_field];
        o.quantity = i[q]; // output key is always quantity
        return o;
      });
    }
  });
  return out;
}

// This event's state lists and totals (from the property kinds in the plan):
var BR_RULES = [];

// GTM custom template setup
//  1. Trigger: Custom Event "view_item", fires only when the Data Layer Variable "bloomreach" is defined.
//  2. Tag: the E3 Bloomreach tag (custom template) reads the Data Layer Variable "bloomreach".
//  3. The tag calls the Web SDK: bloomreach.track('view_item', brPayload(dataLayer.bloomreach, BR_RULES));
//  4. One tag serves every event (it reads bloomreach.event); only the trigger differs per site event.

Use cases

UC-01 — Abandoned cart

Type
marketing

Recovers carts abandoned after an add-to-cart event with no completed checkout.

Requirements
